<style>
:root{
  --bg:#F5F6F8;
  --card:#FFFFFF;
  --border:#E1E4E8;
  --text:#252423;
  --text-soft:#605E5C;
  --blue:#118DFF;
  --blue-dark:#0F6CBD;
  --amber:#FFB900;
  --red:#D13438;
  --green:#107C10;
  --shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.06),0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.04);
}
*{box-sizing:border-box;}
body{
  margin:0;
  font-family:"Segoe UI",-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;
  background:var(--bg);
  color:var(--text);
}
.topbar{
  background:var(--card);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  padding:14px 24px;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
  gap:12px;
  position:sticky;
  top:0;
  z-index:20;
}
.topbar h1{
  font-size:18px;
  margin:0;
  font-weight:600;
}
.topbar .sub{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  margin-top:2px;
}
.controls{
  display:flex;
  gap:10px;
  align-items:center;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
}
.controls label{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  margin-right:4px;
}
input[type=date], input[type=text], select{
  font-family:inherit;
  font-size:13px;
  padding:6px 10px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:4px;
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text);
}
button{
  font-family:inherit;
  font-size:13px;
  padding:7px 14px;
  border-radius:4px;
  border:1px solid var(--blue);
  background:var(--blue);
  color:#fff;
  cursor:pointer;
  font-weight:600;
}
button.secondary{
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--blue);
}
button.ghost{
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  border-color:var(--border);
  font-weight:400;
}
button:hover{ filter:brightness(0.95); }
#fileInput{ display:none; }
#billingFileInput{ display:none; }

.wrap{ padding:20px 24px 60px; max-width:1400px; margin:0 auto; }

.kpis{
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(190px,1fr));
  gap:12px;
  margin-bottom:20px;
}
.kpi{
  background:var(--card);
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:6px;
  padding:14px 16px;
  box-shadow:var(--shadow);
}
.kpi .val{ font-size:26px; font-weight:700; line-height:1.1;}
.kpi .lbl{ font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); margin-top:4px;}
.kpi.blue .val{ color:var(--blue-dark); }
.kpi.red .val{ color:var(--red); }
.kpi.green .val{ color:var(--green); }
.kpi.amber .val{ color:#B36B00; }

.panel{
  background:var(--card);
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:6px;
  box-shadow:var(--shadow);
  margin-bottom:20px;
  overflow:hidden;
}
.panel-head{
  padding:12px 16px;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
  gap:8px;
}
.panel-head h2{ font-size:14px; margin:0; font-weight:600; }
.panel-body{ padding:14px 16px; }

table{ width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:13px; }
th{
  text-align:left;
  padding:8px 10px;
  border-bottom:2px solid var(--border);
  color:var(--text-soft);
  font-weight:600;
  font-size:12px;
  text-transform:uppercase;
  letter-spacing:.02em;
  cursor:pointer;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
th:hover{ color:var(--blue-dark); }
td{
  padding:8px 10px;
  border-bottom:1px solid #F0F0F0;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
tr:hover td{ background:#FAFBFC; }
.olt-row{ cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; }
.olt-row td{ background:#F8FAFD; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); }
.olt-row:hover td{ background:#EEF5FF; }
.bar-cell{ display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; min-width:140px; }
.bar-track{ flex:1; height:8px; background:#EDEDED; border-radius:4px; overflow:hidden; min-width:60px; }
.bar-fill{ height:100%; background:var(--blue); border-radius:4px; }
.pill{
  display:inline-block;
  padding:2px 8px;
  border-radius:10px;
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:600;
}
.pill.counter{ background:#FFF4CE; color:#8A6D00; }
.pill.online{ background:#DFF6DD; color:#107C10; }
.pill.na{ background:#F0F0F0; color:#8A8886; }
.followBtn{
  font-size:11px;
  padding:4px 10px;
  border-radius:12px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  font-weight:600;
  cursor:pointer;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
.followBtn:hover{ border-color:var(--blue); color:var(--blue-dark); }
.followBtn.done{
  background:#DFF6DD;
  color:var(--green);
  border-color:#B3E6B3;
}
.remarksInput{
  font-size:12px;
  padding:5px 8px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:4px;
  width:160px;
}

.customer-block{ display:none; }
.customer-block.open{ display:block; }
.customer-block .panel-body{ background:#FCFCFD; }

.note{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  background:#FFF9E6;
  border:1px solid #FFE9A8;
  border-radius:4px;
  padding:8px 12px;
  margin-bottom:12px;
}
.empty{ text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--text-soft); font-size:13px; }
.footer-row{ display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-top:10px; font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); }
.tag-source{ font-size:10px; padding:1px 6px; border-radius:8px; background:#EEF5FF; color:var(--blue-dark); margin-left:6px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar{ height:8px; width:8px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{ background:#D7D7D7; border-radius:4px; }
.scrollx{ overflow-x:auto; }

/* ---- Login overlay ---- */
#loginOverlay{
  position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(20,20,25,0.55); display:flex;
  align-items:center; justify-content:center; z-index:9999;
}
#loginBox{
  background:var(--card); border-radius:12px; padding:28px 32px; width:320px;
  box-shadow:0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
}
#loginBox h2{ margin:0 0 4px 0; font-size:18px; }
#loginBox p{ margin:0 0 16px 0; font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); }
#loginBox label{ font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); display:block; margin-bottom:4px; }
#loginBox input{ width:100%; padding:8px 10px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:12px; font-size:14px; }
#loginBox button{ width:100%; padding:9px; border:none; border-radius:6px; background:var(--blue); color:#fff; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer; }
#loginBox button:hover{ background:var(--blue-dark); }
#loginErr{ color:var(--red); font-size:12px; margin-top:-6px; margin-bottom:10px; min-height:14px; }
#staffBadge{ font-size:12px; background:#EEF6FF; color:var(--blue-dark); padding:5px 10px; border-radius:14px; font-weight:600; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; }
#staffBadge button{ border:none; background:transparent; color:var(--red); cursor:pointer; font-size:11px; font-weight:600; }

/* ---- High risk panel ---- */
.hr-badge{ background:#FDE7E9; color:var(--red); border:1px solid #F3B7BB; border-radius:10px; padding:1px 6px; font-size:10px; font-weight:700; margin-left:6px; }
.risk-hi{ color:var(--red); font-weight:700; }
.risk-med{ color:#C97A00; font-weight:700; }
#highRiskPanel .panel-head{ background:#FFF4F4; }
#highRiskPanel h2{ color:var(--red); }

/* ---- Remarks dropdown ---- */
select.remarksInput{
  padding:5px 6px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:6px; font-size:12px; max-width:190px;
}

/* ---- Summary report panel ---- */
#summaryPanel table{ font-size:12px; }
#summaryPanel td, #summaryPanel th{ padding:6px 8px; }
.cat-pill{ display:inline-block; background:#F3F2F1; border-radius:10px; padding:1px 7px; font-size:10px; margin:1px 2px; color:var(--text-soft); }

/* ---- Top-level section tabs ---- */
.mainTabs{
  display:flex; gap:4px; padding:0 24px; background:var(--card);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); position:sticky; top:64px; z-index:19; overflow-x:auto;
}
.mainTab{
  background:transparent; border:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-radius:0;
  color:var(--text-soft); font-weight:600; font-size:13px; padding:12px 16px; cursor:pointer; white-space:nowrap;
}
.mainTab:hover{ color:var(--blue-dark); filter:none; }
.mainTab.active{ color:var(--blue-dark); border-bottom-color:var(--blue); }
.tabSection{ display:none; }
.tabSection.active{ display:block; }

/* ---- Year Start Disable tab (rules shared with Unpaid Follow-up tab) ---- */
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi-row, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi-row{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr); gap:14px; margin-bottom:18px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi{ background:var(--card); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:4px; padding:14px 16px; border-top:3px solid var(--blue); }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi.remaining, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi.remaining{ border-top-color:var(--red); }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi.matched, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi.matched{ border-top-color:var(--green); }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi.pct, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi.pct{ border-top-color:var(--amber); }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi .label, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi .label{ font-size:11px; color:var(--text-soft); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.4px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi .value, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi .value{ font-size:24px; font-weight:700; margin-top:4px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-kpi .foot, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi .foot{ font-size:11px; color:var(--text-soft); margin-top:2px; }
@media(max-width:980px){ #tabYSD .ysd-kpi-row, #tabUnpaid .ysd-kpi-row{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);} }
#tabYSD .ysd-grid2, #tabUnpaid .ysd-grid2{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr; gap:18px; }
@media(max-width:980px){ #tabYSD .ysd-grid2, #tabUnpaid .ysd-grid2{grid-template-columns:1fr;} }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-pill, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-pill{ display:inline-block; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:10px; font-size:11px; font-weight:600; }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-SKGD01, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-SKGD01{ background:#DEECF9; color:#005A9E; }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-VMAD01, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-VMAD01{ background:#DFF6DD; color:#107C10; }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-RISH01, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-RISH01{ background:#FFF4CE; color:#8A6D00; }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-DMLI, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-DMLI{ background:#F3E9FB; color:#5C2D91; }
#tabYSD .ysd-olt-UNKNOWN, #tabUnpaid .ysd-olt-UNKNOWN{ background:#F3F2F1; color:var(--text-soft); }
#tabYSD .ysd-status-Matched, #tabUnpaid .ysd-status-Matched{ color:var(--green); font-weight:600; }
#tabYSD .ysd-status-Remaining, #tabUnpaid .ysd-status-Remaining{ color:var(--red); font-weight:600; }
#tabYSD .ysd-bar-track, #tabUnpaid .ysd-bar-track{ background:#F3F2F1; border-radius:8px; height:8px; width:100%; overflow:hidden; }
#tabYSD .ysd-bar-fill, #tabUnpaid .ysd-bar-fill{ height:100%; background:var(--blue); }
#tabYSD .ysd-toolbar, #tabUnpaid .ysd-toolbar{ display:flex; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:center; margin-bottom:12px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-toolbar input, #tabYSD .ysd-toolbar select, #tabUnpaid .ysd-toolbar input, #tabUnpaid .ysd-toolbar select{ font-family:inherit; font-size:12.5px; padding:6px 10px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:4px; background:#fff; }
#tabYSD .ysd-toolbar input, #tabUnpaid .ysd-toolbar input{ flex:1; min-width:160px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-table-scroll, #tabUnpaid .ysd-table-scroll{ max-height:420px; overflow-y:auto; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:4px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-field, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-field{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-field label, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-field label{ font-size:11px; color:var(--text-soft); font-weight:600; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-field input[type=file], #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-field input[type=file]{ font-size:12px; border:1px dashed var(--border); border-radius:4px; padding:8px; background:#FAFAFA; width:280px; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-status, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-status{ font-size:12px; margin-top:10px; padding:8px 10px; border-radius:4px; display:none; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-status.ok, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-status.ok{ background:#DFF6DD; color:#107C10; display:block; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-status.err, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-status.err{ background:#FDE7E9; color:#D13438; display:block; }
#tabYSD .ysd-import-status.info, #tabUnpaid .ysd-import-status.info{ background:#DEECF9; color:#005A9E; display:block; }
#tabYSD .ysd-data-source-tag, #tabUnpaid .ysd-data-source-tag{ font-size:11px; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:10px; background:#F3F2F1; color:var(--text-soft); font-weight:600; }
#tabYSD .ysd-data-source-tag.imported, #tabUnpaid .ysd-data-source-tag.imported{ background:#DFF6DD; color:#107C10; }
#tabYSD .ysd-num, #tabUnpaid .ysd-num{ text-align:right; font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums; }
#tabYSD canvas, #tabUnpaid canvas{ max-width:100%; }

/* ---- Billing-matched (green) username highlight, used across tabs ---- */
.paid-user{ color:var(--green); font-weight:700; }
.paid-badge{ display:inline-block; font-size:10px; font-weight:700; color:#107C10; background:#DFF6DD; border-radius:8px; padding:0 6px; margin-left:5px; }

/* ---- Customer list modal ---- */
#custModalOverlay{
  position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(20,20,25,0.55); display:none;
  align-items:flex-start; justify-content:center; z-index:9998; padding:40px 20px; overflow-y:auto;
}
#custModalOverlay.open{ display:flex; }
#custModalBox{
  background:var(--card); border-radius:8px; width:100%; max-width:1100px;
  box-shadow:0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); max-height:calc(100vh - 80px); display:flex; flex-direction:column;
}
#custModalBox .panel-head{ flex:none; }
#custModalBox .panel-body{ overflow-y:auto; }
#custModalClose{
  background:transparent; border:none; color:var(--text-soft); font-size:20px; font-weight:400;
  cursor:pointer; padding:2px 8px; line-height:1;
}
#custModalClose:hover{ color:var(--red); filter:none; }
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div id="loginOverlay">
  <div id="loginBox">
    <h2>Staff Login</h2>
    <p>Expiry Tracker access garna staff ID ra password halnus.</p>
    <label for="loginUser">Staff ID</label>
    <input type="text" id="loginUser" autocomplete="username" placeholder="e.g. luksha">
    <label for="loginPass">Password</label>
    <input type="password" id="loginPass" autocomplete="current-password" placeholder="••••••••">
    <div id="loginErr"></div>
    <button id="loginBtn">Login</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="topbar">
  <div>
    <h1>Expiry Tracker — Shuklagandaki (Region 5)</h1>
    <div class="sub" id="dataMeta">Loading…</div>
  </div>
  <div class="controls">
    <div>
      <label for="uptoDate">Expire भएका (up to)</label>
      <input type="date" id="uptoDate">
    </div>
    <div>
      <label for="oltFilter">OLT</label>
      <select id="oltFilter"><option value="ALL">All OLTs</option></select>
    </div>
    <button class="secondary" id="importBtn">Excel Import</button>
    <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
    <button class="secondary" id="billingImportBtn">Billing Import</button>
    <input type="file" id="billingFileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
    <button class="ghost" id="exportAllBtn">Export CSV</button>
    <span id="staffBadge" style="display:none;"></span>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="mainTabs">
  <button class="mainTab active" data-tab="tabOverview">Overview</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabExpiry">Expiry Users</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabHighRisk">High Risk Users</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabYSD">Year Start Disable</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabUnpaid">Unpaid Follow-up</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabSummary">Summary Reports</button>
</div>

<div class="wrap">

  <div class="note" id="paymentNote">
    Yo dataset ma <b>Counter / Online payment</b> field xaina — jun file ma <i>Payment Mode / Payment Type / Channel</i> jasto column huncha, tyo import garepachi automatic detect भएर breakdown देखिन्छ।
  </div>

  <!-- ============ OVERVIEW TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection active" id="tabOverview">
    <div class="kpis" id="kpiRow"></div>
    <div class="note" id="billingNote" style="display:none;"></div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>OLT-wise Expired Users (till selected date)</h2>
        <span id="oltPanelSub" class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table id="oltTable">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th data-k="olt">OLT</th>
              <th data-k="count">Total User</th>
              <th data-k="followup">Total Follow-up</th>
              <th data-k="pending">Bill Pending (NPR)</th>
              <th data-k="revenue">Revenue at Stake (NPR)</th>
              <th data-k="counter">Counter</th>
              <th data-k="online">Online</th>
              <th data-k="share">Share</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="oltTbody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
        <div class="footer-row"><span>OLT row click garera tyo OLT ko customer list modal ma herna sakincha.</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ EXPIRY USERS TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabExpiry">
    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>All Expiring Users</h2>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Search username/plan..." class="searchBox" id="expiryListSearch" style="width:200px;">
          <span id="expiryListSub" class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
          <button class="ghost" id="expiryListExport">Export CSV</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th><th>Expiry Date</th><th>Plan</th><th>Bandwidth</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Payment</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="expiryListBody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ HIGH RISK USERS TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabHighRisk">
    <div class="panel" id="highRiskPanel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>🔴 High Risk — Not Renewed (Habitual Late Payers)</h2>
        <span class="sub" id="highRiskSub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Risk Score</th><th>Type</th><th>Status</th><th>Fore. Revenue</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="highRiskBody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ YEAR START DISABLE TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabYSD">

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>Year Start Disable — Import <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);font-weight:400;">Uses the shared Billing Import data (top of page) to mark Matched vs Remaining</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="ysd-toolbar">
          <div class="ysd-import-field">
            <label>Year Start Disable list (.xlsx)</label>
            <input type="file" id="ysdFileDisable" accept=".xlsx,.xls">
          </div>
          <button class="secondary" id="ysdImportBtn">Import &amp; Recompute</button>
          <button class="ghost" id="ysdResetBtn">Reset to Default</button>
          <span class="ysd-data-source-tag" id="ysdDataSourceTag">Default sample data</span>
        </div>
        <div class="ysd-import-status" id="ysdImportStatus"></div>
        <div class="ysd-toolbar" style="margin-top:10px;">
          <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);font-weight:600;">EXPORT:</span>
          <button class="ghost" id="ysdExportCsvBtn">Export CSV</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="ysd-kpi-row">
      <div class="ysd-kpi">
        <div class="label">Total Disabled (Year Start)</div>
        <div class="value" id="ysdKpiTotal">–</div>
        <div class="foot">Unique usernames</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ysd-kpi matched">
        <div class="label">Matched / Renewed Since</div>
        <div class="value" id="ysdKpiMatched">–</div>
        <div class="foot">Found in shared Billing Import</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ysd-kpi remaining">
        <div class="label">Still Remaining</div>
        <div class="value" id="ysdKpiRemaining">–</div>
        <div class="foot">No billing record yet</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ysd-kpi pct">
        <div class="label">Overall Match Rate</div>
        <div class="value" id="ysdKpiPct">–</div>
        <div class="foot">Winback conversion so far</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ysd-kpi">
        <div class="label">Oldest Remaining Vintage</div>
        <div class="value" id="ysdKpiOldest">–</div>
        <div class="foot">Earliest create-year still stuck</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head"><h2>OLT-wise Match Summary <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">Total → Matched vs Remaining</span></h2></div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>OLT</th><th class="ysd-num">Total</th><th class="ysd-num">Matched</th><th class="ysd-num">Remaining</th>
            <th class="ysd-num">Match %</th><th style="width:180px">Progress</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="ysdOltTable"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="ysd-grid2">
      <div class="panel">
        <div class="panel-head"><h2>Remaining — Create Year Category <span class="sub" id="ysdRemTag" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span></h2></div>
        <div class="panel-body"><canvas id="ysdYearChart" height="220"></canvas></div>
      </div>
      <div class="panel">
        <div class="panel-head"><h2>Matched vs Remaining <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">by OLT</span></h2></div>
        <div class="panel-body"><canvas id="ysdOltChart" height="220"></canvas></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head"><h2>User-level Detail <span class="sub" id="ysdDetailCount" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span></h2></div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="ysd-toolbar">
          <input type="text" id="ysdSearchBox" placeholder="Search username...">
          <select id="ysdOltFilterLocal"><option value="">All OLTs</option></select>
          <select id="ysdStatusFilter">
            <option value="">All Status</option>
            <option value="Matched">Matched</option>
            <option value="Remaining">Remaining</option>
          </select>
          <select id="ysdYearFilter"><option value="">All Create Years</option></select>
        </div>
        <div class="ysd-table-scroll">
          <table>
            <thead><tr>
              <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Created</th><th>Expiry</th>
              <th class="ysd-num">Fcst Revenue</th><th>Status</th>
            </tr></thead>
            <tbody id="ysdDetailTable"></tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ UNPAID FOLLOW-UP TAB (new installations, NOT PAID) ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabUnpaid">

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>Unpaid Follow-up — Import <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);font-weight:400;">New Installation file bata PAYMENT STATUS = NOT PAID matra tanincha. Green tick shared Billing Import bata match huncha.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="ysd-toolbar">
          <div class="ysd-import-field">
            <label>New Installation / Order list (.xlsx)</label>
            <input type="file" id="unpaidFileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls">
          </div>
          <button class="secondary" id="unpaidImportBtn">Import &amp; Recompute</button>
          <button class="ghost" id="unpaidResetBtn">Clear</button>
          <span class="ysd-data-source-tag" id="unpaidDataSourceTag">No file imported yet</span>
        </div>
        <div class="ysd-import-status" id="unpaidImportStatus"></div>
        <div class="ysd-toolbar" style="margin-top:10px;">
          <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);font-weight:600;">EXPORT:</span>
          <button class="ghost" id="unpaidExportCsvBtn">Export CSV</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="kpis" id="unpaidKpiRow"></div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head"><h2>Staff-wise Unpaid Summary <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">Marketed By ko basis ma — Billing bata match bhaisakeka hataera</span></h2></div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Marketed By (Staff)</th><th class="ysd-num">Unpaid Count</th><th class="ysd-num">Billing Matched</th>
            <th class="ysd-num">Still Pending</th><th class="ysd-num">Amount Pending (Rs)</th><th class="ysd-num">Followed-up</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="unpaidStaffTable"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head"><h2>User-level Detail <span class="sub" id="unpaidDetailCount" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span></h2></div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="ysd-toolbar">
          <input type="text" id="unpaidSearchBox" placeholder="Search username/staff...">
          <select id="unpaidOltFilter"><option value="">All OLTs</option></select>
          <select id="unpaidStaffFilter"><option value="">All Staff</option></select>
          <select id="unpaidStatusFilter">
            <option value="">All Billing Status</option>
            <option value="Matched">Matched (Paid)</option>
            <option value="Pending">Pending</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="ysd-table-scroll">
          <table>
            <thead><tr>
              <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Marketed By</th><th>Plan</th><th>Order Date</th>
              <th class="ysd-num">Days Unpaid</th><th class="ysd-num">Amount</th><th>Billing Status</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th>
            </tr></thead>
            <tbody id="unpaidDetailTable"></tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ SUMMARY REPORTS TAB (entire combined report) ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabSummary">

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>Overview Snapshot</h2>
        <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);" id="summarySnapSub"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="kpis" id="kpiRowSummary"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>OLT-wise Breakdown</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>OLT</th><th>Total User</th><th>Total Follow-up</th><th>Bill Pending (NPR)</th>
              <th>Revenue at Stake (NPR)</th><th>Counter</th><th>Online</th><th>Share</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="oltTbodySummary"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>🔴 High Risk Users — Summary</h2>
        <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);" id="highRiskSummarySub"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="kpis" id="highRiskSummaryKpis"></div>
        <div class="scrollx" style="margin-top:12px;">
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>OLT</th><th>High Risk Users</th><th>Followed-up</th><th>Not Followed-up</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody id="highRiskSummaryByOlt"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>💰 Billing Matches by Source</h2>
        <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">Imported billing/payment usernames, broken down by which section they were found in</span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="kpis" id="billingBySourceKpis"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel" id="summaryPanelWrap">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>📊 Staff-wise Summary Report</h2>
        <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">Follow-ups, effort hours, ra collected billing staff anusar</span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body" id="summaryPanel"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

<!-- ============ Customer list modal (opens on OLT row click) ============ -->
<div id="custModalOverlay">
  <div id="custModalBox">
    <div class="panel-head">
      <h2 id="custModalTitle">Customer List</h2>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Search username/plan..." class="searchBox" id="custModalSearch" style="width:200px;">
        <button class="ghost" id="custModalExport">Export CSV</button>
        <button id="custModalClose">&times;</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body">
      <div class="scrollx">
      <table>
        <thead><tr>
          <th>Username</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th><th>Expiry Date</th><th>Plan</th><th>Bandwidth</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Payment</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody id="custModalBody"></tbody>
      </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
let STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL = {}; // id -> minutes (today, from Firebase) — declared early to avoid TDZ issues
let RAW = [];        // active dataset (array of row objects, normalized keys)
let COLMETA = { hasPayment:false, paymentKey:null };
let YSD_DATA = [];              // Year Start Disable dataset: {u, olt, cy, created, expiry, rev} — status computed live from BILLING_PAID
let ysdYearChartInstance = null, ysdOltChartInstance = null;
const YSD_OLT_ORDER = ['SKGD01','VMAD01','RISH01','DMLI','UNKNOWN'];
const YSD_OLT_COLORS = {SKGD01:'#0078D4', VMAD01:'#107C10', RISH01:'#FFB900', DMLI:'#5C2D91', UNKNOWN:'#A19F9D'};
let BILLING_PAID = new Set();   // normalized usernames found in imported billing data
let BILLING_AMOUNT = {};        // normalized username -> paid amount (if billing file has an amount column)
let BILLING_META = { fileName:'', count:0 };
let HR_FOLLOWUPS = {};          // normalized username -> {username, followUp, remarks, followedUpBy, followedUpByName} for High-Risk list
let UNPAID_DATA = [];           // Unpaid Follow-up dataset: {u, olt, marketedBy, plan, created, amt} — status computed live from BILLING_PAID
let UNPAID_FOLLOWUPS = {};      // normalized username -> {username, followUp, remarks, followedUpBy, followedUpByName}

function normUser(u){ return String(u||'').trim().toLowerCase(); }
function isBilled(username){ return BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(username)); }
function billedUsernameHtml(username){
  return isBilled(username)
    ? `<span class="paid-user">${username}</span><span class="paid-badge">BILLED</span>`
    : username;
}

// ---------- OLT extraction ----------
function deriveOLT(username){
  if(!username) return 'UNMAPPED';
  const u = String(username).toLowerCase();
  const suffix = u.includes('_') ? u.split('_').pop() : u;
  if(/skgd|skhd|skga|sskgd/.test(suffix)) return 'SKGD01';
  if(/vmad|vmd|vamd|vmqd/.test(suffix))   return 'VMAD01';
  if(/rish/.test(suffix))                 return 'RISH01';
  return 'OTHER (' + suffix.toUpperCase() + ')';
}

// ---------- normalize a raw imported row into our schema ----------
function findKey(row, patterns){
  const keys = Object.keys(row);
  for(const k of keys){
    const lk = k.toLowerCase();
    for(const p of patterns){
      if(lk.includes(p)) return k;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function normalizeDataset(rows){
  if(!rows.length) return [];
  const sample = rows[0];
  const userKey = findKey(sample, ['username']) || Object.keys(sample)[0];
  const expiryKey = findKey(sample, ['expiry']);
  const revenueKey = findKey(sample, ['revenue']);
  const planKey = findKey(sample, ['plan name','planname','plan']);
  const bandwidthKey = findKey(sample, ['bandwidth']);
  const statusKey = findKey(sample, ['transaction date','status','renew']);
  const paymentKey = findKey(sample, ['payment mode','payment type','payment channel','payment','channel']);

  COLMETA.hasPayment = !!paymentKey;
  COLMETA.paymentKey = paymentKey;

  return rows.map(r=>{
    let expiry = r[expiryKey];
    if(expiry instanceof Date){
      expiry = expiry.toISOString().slice(0,10);
    } else if(typeof expiry === 'number'){
      // excel serial date
      const d = XLSX.SSF.parse_date_code(expiry);
      expiry = d ? `${d.y}-${String(d.m).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.d).padStart(2,'0')}` : '';
    } else if(typeof expiry === 'string' && expiry.length>10){
      expiry = expiry.slice(0,10);
    }
    return {
      username: r[userKey] || '',
      olt: deriveOLT(r[userKey]),
      expiry: expiry || '',
      status: statusKey ? r[statusKey] : '',
      revenue: revenueKey ? (parseFloat(r[revenueKey]) || 0) : 0,
      plan: planKey ? r[planKey] : '',
      bandwidth: bandwidthKey ? r[bandwidthKey] : '',
      payment: paymentKey ? (r[paymentKey] || 'N/A') : 'N/A'
    };
  });
}

function classifyPayment(val){
  const v = String(val || '').toLowerCase();
  if(v.includes('online') || v.includes('esewa') || v.includes('khalti') || v.includes('bank') || v.includes('card')) return 'online';
  if(v.includes('counter') || v.includes('cash')) return 'counter';
  return null;
}

// ---------- rendering ----------
function fmtNum(n){ return Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-IN'); }

function getUptoDate(){
  return document.getElementById('uptoDate').value;
}

function filteredRows(){
  const upto = getUptoDate();
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter').value;
  return RAW.filter(r=>{
    if(!r.expiry) return false;
    if(upto && r.expiry > upto) return false;
    if(oltSel !== 'ALL' && r.olt !== oltSel) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function render(){
  const rows = filteredRows();

  // KPIs
  const totalUsers = rows.length;
  const totalRevenue = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.revenue,0);
  const oltCount = new Set(rows.map(r=>r.olt)).size;
  let counterN=0, onlineN=0, naN=0;
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const c = classifyPayment(r.payment);
    if(c==='counter') counterN++; else if(c==='online') onlineN++; else naN++;
  });

  const followUpTotal = rows.reduce((s,r)=> s + (r.followUp ? 1 : 0), 0);
  const paymentDoneTotal = BILLING_PAID.size ? rows.reduce((s,r)=> s + (BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username)) ? 1 : 0), 0) : 0;

  const kpiRow = document.getElementById('kpiRow');
  const kpiHtml = `
    <div class="kpi red"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalUsers)}</div><div class="lbl">Total Expired Users</div></div>
    <div class="kpi blue"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalRevenue)}</div><div class="lbl">Revenue at Stake (NPR)</div></div>
    <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${oltCount}</div><div class="lbl">OLTs Affected</div></div>
    <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${COLMETA.hasPayment ? (counterN+'/'+onlineN) : '—'}</div><div class="lbl">Counter / Online split</div></div>
    <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${fmtNum(followUpTotal)}</div><div class="lbl">Follow-up Total</div></div>
    <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${BILLING_PAID.size ? fmtNum(paymentDoneTotal) : '—'}</div><div class="lbl">Payment Done</div></div>
  `;
  kpiRow.innerHTML = kpiHtml;
  const kpiRowSummary = document.getElementById('kpiRowSummary');
  if(kpiRowSummary) kpiRowSummary.innerHTML = kpiHtml;
  const summarySnapSub = document.getElementById('summarySnapSub');
  if(summarySnapSub) summarySnapSub.textContent = `Upto: ${getUptoDate() || 'all'} · Filter: ${document.getElementById('oltFilter').value}`;

  document.getElementById('paymentNote').style.display = COLMETA.hasPayment ? 'none' : 'block';

  const billingNote = document.getElementById('billingNote');
  billingNote.style.display = 'block';
  if(BILLING_PAID.size){
    billingNote.textContent = `Billing data loaded: ${BILLING_META.fileName} · ${fmtNum(BILLING_META.count)} unique users. "Payment Done" upar tyahi bata calculate bhako ho.`;
  } else {
    billingNote.textContent = 'Billing data import gareko xaina — "Billing Import" button bata renewal/payment file (jasto USERNAME + RECENT INVOICE RENEW DATE column bhayeko) upload garepachi "Payment Done" count dekhincha.';
  }

  // OLT grouping
  const groups = {};
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    if(!groups[r.olt]) groups[r.olt] = {olt:r.olt, count:0, revenue:0, counter:0, online:0, na:0, followUp:0, pending:0, rows:[]};
    const g = groups[r.olt];
    g.count++; g.revenue += r.revenue; g.rows.push(r);
    const c = classifyPayment(r.payment);
    if(c==='counter') g.counter++; else if(c==='online') g.online++; else g.na++;
    if(r.followUp) g.followUp++;
    if(!BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username))) g.pending += r.revenue;
  });
  const groupArr = Object.values(groups).sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count);
  const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...groupArr.map(g=>g.count));

  const tbody = document.getElementById('oltTbody');
  tbody.innerHTML = '';

  if(groupArr.length===0){
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Selected filter/date ma expired user chaina</td></tr>`;
  }

  groupArr.forEach((g)=>{
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.className = 'olt-row';
    tr.dataset.olt = g.olt;
    const share = ((g.count/totalUsers)*100).toFixed(1);
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>👁 ${g.olt}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
      <td><span class="pill online">${fmtNum(g.followUp)}</span> / ${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.pending)}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.revenue)}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill counter">${g.counter}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill online">${g.online}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
      <td><div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${(g.count/maxCount*100).toFixed(0)}%"></div></div><span>${share}%</span></div></td>
    `;
    tr.addEventListener('click', ()=> openCustomerModal(g));
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });

  // Summary-tab duplicate of the OLT breakdown (read-only, no modal click)
  const tbodySummary = document.getElementById('oltTbodySummary');
  if(tbodySummary){
    tbodySummary.innerHTML = groupArr.length===0
      ? `<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Selected filter/date ma expired user chaina</td></tr>`
      : groupArr.map(g=>{
          const share = ((g.count/totalUsers)*100).toFixed(1);
          return `<tr>
            <td>${g.olt}</td>
            <td>${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
            <td><span class="pill online">${fmtNum(g.followUp)}</span> / ${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
            <td>${fmtNum(g.pending)}</td>
            <td>${fmtNum(g.revenue)}</td>
            <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill counter">${g.counter}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
            <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill online">${g.online}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
            <td><div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${(g.count/maxCount*100).toFixed(0)}%"></div></div><span>${share}%</span></div></td>
          </tr>`;
        }).join('');
  }

  document.getElementById('oltPanelSub').textContent = `Upto: ${getUptoDate() || 'all'} · Filter: ${document.getElementById('oltFilter').value}`;

  renderExpiryList(rows);

  // populate OLT filter options (once meaningful)
  const sel = document.getElementById('oltFilter');
  const existing = new Set(Array.from(sel.options).map(o=>o.value));
  const allOlts = Array.from(new Set(RAW.map(r=>r.olt))).sort();
  allOlts.forEach(o=>{
    if(!existing.has(o)){
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = o; opt.textContent = o;
      sel.appendChild(opt);
    }
  });

  if(typeof renderSummary === 'function') renderSummary();
}

// ---------- All Expiring Users flat list (Expiry Users tab) ----------
let EXPIRY_LIST_ALL = []; // full filtered rows (from render()), before search box narrows it
function renderExpiryList(rows){
  EXPIRY_LIST_ALL = rows;
  const q = (document.getElementById('expiryListSearch').value || '').toLowerCase();
  const list = q ? rows.filter(r=> (r.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (r.plan||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) : rows;
  document.getElementById('expiryListSub').textContent = `${fmtNum(list.length)} user${list.length===1?'':'s'}`;
  const body = document.getElementById('expiryListBody');
  body.innerHTML = list.map(r=>`
    <tr>
      <td>${billedUsernameHtml(r.username)}</td>
      <td>${r.olt}</td>
      <td><button class="followBtn expListFollowBtn ${r.followUp?'done':''}" data-uname="${r.username}">${r.followUp ? '✓ Followed up' : 'Mark follow-up'}</button>${window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ && window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__.some(h=>normUser(h.Username)===normUser(r.username)) ? '<span class="hr-badge">HIGH RISK</span>' : ''}</td>
      <td>${r.followUp ? remarksSelectHtml(r.username, r.remarks) : ''}</td>
      <td>${r.expiry}</td>
      <td>${r.plan||''}</td>
      <td>${r.bandwidth||''}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(r.revenue)}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? (r.payment||'N/A') : '<span class="pill na">N/A</span>'}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">No records</td></tr>`;
}
document.getElementById('expiryListSearch').addEventListener('input', ()=> renderExpiryList(EXPIRY_LIST_ALL));
document.getElementById('expiryListBody').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('expListFollowBtn')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const rowObj = RAW.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);
    if(rowObj){
      rowObj.followUp = !rowObj.followUp;
      if(!rowObj.followUp){ rowObj.remarks = ''; rowObj.followedUpBy=null; rowObj.followedUpByName=null; }
      else { rowObj.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rowObj.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name; }
      renderExpiryList(EXPIRY_LIST_ALL);
      fbSaveFollowup(rowObj.username, rowObj.followUp, rowObj.remarks, 'main');
      renderSummary();
    }
  }
});
document.getElementById('expiryListBody').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('remarksInput')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); e.target.value=''; return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const rowObj = RAW.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);
    if(rowObj){
      rowObj.remarks = e.target.value;
      rowObj.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id;
      rowObj.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
      fbSaveFollowup(rowObj.username, rowObj.followUp, rowObj.remarks, 'main');
      renderSummary();
    }
  }
});
document.getElementById('expiryListExport').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  exportCSV(EXPIRY_LIST_ALL, 'all_expiring_users.csv');
});

// ---------- Customer list modal (opens on OLT row click) ----------
let MODAL_GROUP = null;   // {olt, rows, ...}
let MODAL_LIST = [];      // currently displayed (possibly search-filtered) list

function openCustomerModal(g){
  MODAL_GROUP = g;
  document.getElementById('custModalTitle').textContent = `${g.olt} — Customer List (${g.count})`;
  document.getElementById('custModalSearch').value = '';
  renderModalCust(g.rows);
  document.getElementById('custModalOverlay').classList.add('open');
}
function closeCustomerModal(){
  document.getElementById('custModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
  MODAL_GROUP = null;
}
document.getElementById('custModalClose').addEventListener('click', closeCustomerModal);
document.getElementById('custModalOverlay').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.id === 'custModalOverlay') closeCustomerModal();
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{
  if(e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('custModalOverlay').classList.contains('open')) closeCustomerModal();
});

function renderModalCust(list){
  MODAL_LIST = list;
  const custBody = document.getElementById('custModalBody');
  custBody.innerHTML = list.map(r=>`
    <tr>
      <td>${billedUsernameHtml(r.username)}</td>
      <td><button class="followBtn ${r.followUp?'done':''}" data-uname="${r.username}">${r.followUp ? '✓ Followed up' : 'Mark follow-up'}</button>${window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ && window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__.some(h=>normUser(h.Username)===normUser(r.username)) ? '<span class="hr-badge">HIGH RISK</span>' : ''}</td>
      <td>${r.followUp ? remarksSelectHtml(r.username, r.remarks) : ''}</td>
      <td>${r.expiry}</td>
      <td>${r.plan||''}</td>
      <td>${r.bandwidth||''}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(r.revenue)}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? (r.payment||'N/A') : '<span class="pill na">N/A</span>'}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">No records</td></tr>`;
}

document.getElementById('custModalBody').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(!MODAL_GROUP) return;
  if(e.target.classList.contains('followBtn')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const rowObj = MODAL_GROUP.rows.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);
    if(rowObj){
      rowObj.followUp = !rowObj.followUp;
      if(!rowObj.followUp){ rowObj.remarks = ''; rowObj.followedUpBy=null; rowObj.followedUpByName=null; }
      else { rowObj.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rowObj.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name; }
      renderModalCust(MODAL_LIST);
      fbSaveFollowup(rowObj.username, rowObj.followUp, rowObj.remarks, 'main');
      renderSummary();
    }
  }
});
document.getElementById('custModalBody').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  if(!MODAL_GROUP) return;
  if(e.target.classList.contains('remarksInput')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); e.target.value=''; return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const rowObj = MODAL_GROUP.rows.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);
    if(rowObj){
      rowObj.remarks = e.target.value;
      rowObj.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id;
      rowObj.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
      fbSaveFollowup(rowObj.username, rowObj.followUp, rowObj.remarks, 'main');
      renderSummary();
    }
  }
});
document.getElementById('custModalSearch').addEventListener('input', (e)=>{
  if(!MODAL_GROUP) return;
  const q = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
  renderModalCust(MODAL_GROUP.rows.filter(r=> (r.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (r.plan||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
});
document.getElementById('custModalExport').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  if(!MODAL_GROUP) return;
  exportCSV(MODAL_GROUP.rows, MODAL_GROUP.olt + '_expired_customers.csv');
});

// ---------- Top-level tab switching ----------
document.querySelectorAll('.mainTab').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.mainTab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tabSection').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById(btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
  });
});

function exportCSV(rows, filename){
  const headers = ['Username','Follow-up','Remarks','Expiry Date','Plan','Bandwidth','Revenue','Payment','OLT'];
  const lines = [headers.join(',')];
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    lines.push([r.username, r.followUp ? 'Yes' : 'No', `"${(r.remarks||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`, r.expiry, `"${(r.plan||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`, r.bandwidth, r.revenue, r.payment, r.olt].join(','));
  });
  const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], {type:'text/csv'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename; a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

// ---------- Firebase sync (follow-up + remarks) ----------
function fbSaveFollowup(username, followUp, remarks, kind){
  if(!fbdb){ setSyncStatus('Offline mode (Firebase not loaded)', true); return; }
  const basePath = (kind === 'highrisk') ? 'expiryTracker/highRiskFollowups' : (kind === 'unpaid') ? 'expiryTracker/unpaidFollowups' : FB_PATH;
  try{
    const key = fbKey(username);
    fbdb.ref(basePath + '/' + key).set({
      username: username,
      followUp: !!followUp,
      remarks: remarks || '',
      followedUpBy: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.id : null,
      followedUpByName: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.name : null,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    }).then(()=> setSyncStatus('Synced ✓')).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Firebase save failed', err);
      setSyncStatus('Sync failed — check Firebase rules', true);
    });
    // also log an immutable activity event for audit trail
    if(CURRENT_STAFF){
      fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/activityLog').push({
        staff: CURRENT_STAFF.id, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name, username, followUp: !!followUp,
        remarks: remarks || '', kind: kind || 'main', ts: Date.now()
      });
    }
  }catch(err){
    console.error('Firebase save error', err);
    setSyncStatus('Sync failed', true);
  }
}

function fbLoadAndMerge(){
  if(!fbdb){ setSyncStatus('Offline mode (Firebase not loaded)', true); return; }
  setSyncStatus('Syncing…');
  try{
    fbdb.ref(FB_PATH).once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val() || {};
      const map = {};
      Object.values(data).forEach(v=>{ if(v && v.username) map[v.username] = v; });
      RAW.forEach(r=>{
        const rec = map[r.username];
        if(rec){
          r.followUp = !!rec.followUp; r.remarks = rec.remarks || '';
          r.followedUpBy = rec.followedUpBy || null;
          r.followedUpByName = rec.followedUpByName || null;
        }
      });
      setSyncStatus('Synced with Firebase ✓');
      render();
      renderSummary();
    }).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Firebase load failed', err);
      setSyncStatus('Firebase load failed — offline mode', true);
    });
  }catch(err){
    console.error('Firebase load error', err);
    setSyncStatus('Offline mode', true);
  }
  // load high-risk followups
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/highRiskFollowups').once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val() || {};
      HR_FOLLOWUPS = {};
      Object.values(data).forEach(v=>{ if(v && v.username) HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(v.username)] = v; });
      renderHighRisk();
      renderSummary();
    }).catch(err=> console.error('High-risk load failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('High-risk load error', err); }
  // load unpaid follow-ups
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/unpaidFollowups').once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val() || {};
      UNPAID_FOLLOWUPS = {};
      Object.values(data).forEach(v=>{ if(v && v.username) UNPAID_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(v.username)] = v; });
      renderUnpaid();
    }).catch(err=> console.error('Unpaid follow-up load failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('Unpaid follow-up load error', err); }
  // load imported billing data (shared Billing Import) — persists until re-imported/reset
  fbLoadBillingData();
  // load imported unpaid follow-up dataset (New Installation file) — persists until re-imported/reset
  fbLoadUnpaidData();
}

// ---------- Firebase sync (Billing Import data — shared BILLING_PAID/BILLING_AMOUNT/BILLING_META) ----------
function fbSaveBillingData(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/billingImportData').set({
      fileName: BILLING_META.fileName || '',
      count: BILLING_META.count || 0,
      paidUsers: Array.from(BILLING_PAID),
      amounts: BILLING_AMOUNT || {},
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
      updatedBy: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.name : null
    }).catch(err=> console.error('Billing data save failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('Billing data save error', err); }
}

function fbLoadBillingData(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/billingImportData').once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val();
      if(!data) return; // nothing saved yet — keep current in-memory state
      BILLING_PAID = new Set(data.paidUsers || []);
      BILLING_AMOUNT = data.amounts || {};
      BILLING_META = { fileName: data.fileName || '', count: data.count || BILLING_PAID.size };
      render();
      renderHighRisk();
      renderYSD();
      renderUnpaid();
      renderSummary();
    }).catch(err=> console.error('Billing data load failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('Billing data load error', err); }
}

// ---------- Firebase sync (Unpaid Follow-up import dataset — UNPAID_DATA) ----------
function fbSaveUnpaidData(fileName){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/unpaidImportData').set({
      fileName: fileName || '',
      data: UNPAID_DATA,
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
      updatedBy: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.name : null
    }).catch(err=> console.error('Unpaid import data save failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('Unpaid import data save error', err); }
}

function fbLoadUnpaidData(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/unpaidImportData').once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val();
      if(!data || !Array.isArray(data.data) || !data.data.length) return; // nothing saved yet
      UNPAID_DATA = data.data;
      const tag = document.getElementById('unpaidDataSourceTag');
      if(tag){
        tag.textContent = 'Imported · ' + (data.fileName || 'synced from Firebase');
        tag.classList.add('imported');
      }
      renderUnpaid();
    }).catch(err=> console.error('Unpaid import data load failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('Unpaid import data load error', err); }
}

function fbClearUnpaidData(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  try{ fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/unpaidImportData').remove().catch(err=> console.error('Unpaid import data clear failed', err)); }
  catch(err){ console.error('Unpaid import data clear error', err); }
}

function setSyncStatus(msg, isError){
  let el = document.getElementById('syncStatus');
  if(!el){
    el = document.createElement('span');
    el.id = 'syncStatus';
    el.style.marginLeft = '8px';
    el.style.fontSize = '11px';
    document.getElementById('dataMeta').appendChild(el);
  }
  el.textContent = ' · ' + msg;
  el.style.color = isError ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--green)';
}

// ---------- file import ----------
document.getElementById('importBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=> document.getElementById('fileInput').click());
document.getElementById('fileInput').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (evt)=>{
    const data = new Uint8Array(evt.target.result);
    const wb = XLSX.read(data, {type:'array', cellDates:true});
    const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    // find header row: try row 0 first, else scan for a row containing 'Username'
    let json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {defval:''});
    if(json.length && !findKey(json[0], ['username'])){
      // try re-parsing with header detection by scanning rows
      const raw = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {header:1, defval:''});
      let headerRowIdx = raw.findIndex(row => row.some(c => String(c).toLowerCase().includes('username')));
      if(headerRowIdx >= 0){
        json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {range:headerRowIdx, defval:''});
      }
    }
    RAW = normalizeDataset(json);
    document.getElementById('dataMeta').textContent = `Imported: ${file.name} · ${RAW.length} records`;
    setDefaultUptoDate();
    render();
    fbLoadAndMerge();
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('billingImportBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=> document.getElementById('billingFileInput').click());
document.getElementById('billingFileInput').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev)=>{
    const data = new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
    const wb = XLSX.read(data, {type:'array', cellDates:true});
    const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    let json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {defval:''});
    if(!json.length || !findKey(json[0], ['username'])){
      const raw = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {header:1, defval:''});
      const headerRowIdx = raw.findIndex(row => row.some(c => String(c).toLowerCase().includes('username')));
      if(headerRowIdx >= 0){
        json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {range:headerRowIdx, defval:''});
      }
    }
    if(!json.length){
      BILLING_META = { fileName:file.name, count:0 };
      alert('Billing file ma USERNAME column vetiena.');
      return;
    }
    const userKey = findKey(json[0], ['username']);
    if(!userKey){
      alert('Billing file ma USERNAME column vetiena.');
      return;
    }
    const amountKey = findKey(json[0], ['paid amount','amount paid','recharge amount','invoice amount','amount']);
    BILLING_PAID = new Set();
    BILLING_AMOUNT = {};
    json.forEach(r=>{
      const u = normUser(r[userKey]);
      if(!u) return;
      BILLING_PAID.add(u);
      if(amountKey){
        const amt = parseFloat(String(r[amountKey]).replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,''));
        if(!isNaN(amt)) BILLING_AMOUNT[u] = amt;
      }
    });
    BILLING_META = { fileName:file.name, count:BILLING_PAID.size };
    render();
    renderHighRisk();
    renderYSD();
    renderUnpaid();
    renderSummary();
    fbSaveBillingData();
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('exportAllBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  exportCSV(filteredRows(), 'expired_users_all.csv');
});

document.getElementById('uptoDate').addEventListener('change', render);
document.getElementById('oltFilter').addEventListener('change', ()=>{
  render();
  renderHighRisk();
  renderSummary();
});

function setDefaultUptoDate(){
  const today = new Date();
  const iso = today.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  document.getElementById('uptoDate').value = iso;
}

// ---------- load embedded default dataset ----------
function loadEmbedded(){
  const embedded = window.__EMBEDDED_DATA__ || [];
  const rows = embedded.map(r=>({
    Username: r.Username, 'Expiry Date': r['Expiry Date'], 'Transaction Date': r['Transaction Date'],
    Revenue: r.Revenue, 'Plan Name': r.PlanName, Bandwidth: r.Bandwidth
  }));
  RAW = normalizeDataset(rows);
  document.getElementById('dataMeta').textContent = `Default dataset loaded · ${RAW.length} records (aaja samma expire filter le 'Not Renewed' user haru dekhauxa)`;
  setDefaultUptoDate();
  render();
  fbLoadAndMerge();
}
loadEmbedded();
renderHighRisk();

// ---------- Staff login flow ----------
function doLogin(){
  const idRaw = document.getElementById('loginUser').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const pass = document.getElementById('loginPass').value;
  const rec = STAFF_CREDENTIALS[idRaw];
  const errEl = document.getElementById('loginErr');
  if(!rec || rec.pass !== pass){
    errEl.textContent = 'Galat Staff ID ya Password.';
    return;
  }
  errEl.textContent = '';
  CURRENT_STAFF = { id: idRaw, name: rec.name, role: rec.role };
  SESSION_START = Date.now();
  document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
  const badge = document.getElementById('staffBadge');
  badge.style.display = 'inline-flex';
  badge.innerHTML = `👤 ${CURRENT_STAFF.name} <button id="logoutBtn">Logout</button>`;
  document.getElementById('logoutBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    stopHeartbeat();
    CURRENT_STAFF = null;
    document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('loginUser').value='';
    document.getElementById('loginPass').value='';
    badge.style.display = 'none';
  });
  startHeartbeat();
  fbLogSession('login');
  renderSummary();
}
document.getElementById('loginBtn').addEventListener('click', doLogin);
document.getElementById('loginPass').addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });
document.getElementById('loginUser').addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });

// ---------- Time / effort tracking ----------
let HEARTBEAT_TIMER = null;
function todayStr(){ return new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); }
function startHeartbeat(){
  if(HEARTBEAT_TIMER) return;
  HEARTBEAT_TIMER = setInterval(()=>{
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF || !fbdb) return;
    if(document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return; // only count active/foreground time
    const path = `expiryTracker/staffTime/${todayStr()}/${CURRENT_STAFF.id}`;
    fbdb.ref(path).transaction(cur=>{
      const c = cur || { minutes:0, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name };
      c.minutes = (c.minutes||0) + 1;
      c.name = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
      c.lastActive = Date.now();
      return c;
    });
  }, 60000); // 1 heartbeat per minute of active foreground time
}
function stopHeartbeat(){
  if(HEARTBEAT_TIMER){ clearInterval(HEARTBEAT_TIMER); HEARTBEAT_TIMER = null; }
  fbLogSession('logout');
}
function fbLogSession(kind){
  if(!fbdb || !CURRENT_STAFF) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/sessionLog').push({
      staff: CURRENT_STAFF.id, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name, kind, ts: Date.now()
    });
  }catch(err){ console.error('session log failed', err); }
}
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', ()=>{ if(CURRENT_STAFF) fbLogSession('close'); });

// ---------- High-risk panel ----------
function renderHighRisk(){
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter') ? document.getElementById('oltFilter').value : 'ALL';
  const data = (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).filter(u=> oltSel==='ALL' || u.OLT===oltSel);
  const sorted = [...data].sort((a,b)=> parseFloat(b.RiskScore||0) - parseFloat(a.RiskScore||0));
  document.getElementById('highRiskSub').textContent = `${sorted.length} habitual/high-risk users (curated list) — Filter: ${oltSel}`;
  const tbody = document.getElementById('highRiskBody');
  function rowHtml(u){
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(u.Username)] || {};
    const scoreClass = parseFloat(u.RiskScore||0) >= 90 ? 'risk-hi' : 'risk-med';
    return `
      <tr>
        <td>${billedUsernameHtml(u.Username)}</td>
        <td>${u.OLT}</td>
        <td class="${scoreClass}">${u.RiskScore}</td>
        <td>${u.Type||''}</td>
        <td>${u.Status||''}</td>
        <td>${u.ForeRevenue||''}</td>
        <td><button class="followBtn hrFollowBtn ${rec.followUp?'done':''}" data-uname="${u.Username}">${rec.followUp ? '✓ Followed up' : 'Mark follow-up'}</button></td>
        <td>${rec.followUp ? remarksSelectHtml(u.Username, rec.remarks) : ''}</td>
      </tr>`;
  }
  tbody.innerHTML = sorted.map(rowHtml).join('');

  // Summary-tab aggregate recap (counts only — NOT a per-user list)
  const subSummary = document.getElementById('highRiskSummarySub');
  if(subSummary) subSummary.textContent = `Filter: ${oltSel}`;
  const kpisSummary = document.getElementById('highRiskSummaryKpis');
  if(kpisSummary){
    const followedUp = sorted.filter(u=> (HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(u.Username)]||{}).followUp).length;
    const highScore = sorted.filter(u=> parseFloat(u.RiskScore||0) >= 90).length;
    kpisSummary.innerHTML = `
      <div class="kpi red"><div class="val">${fmtNum(sorted.length)}</div><div class="lbl">Total High Risk Users</div></div>
      <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${fmtNum(highScore)}</div><div class="lbl">Risk Score ≥ 90</div></div>
      <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${fmtNum(followedUp)}</div><div class="lbl">Followed-up</div></div>
      <div class="kpi blue"><div class="val">${fmtNum(sorted.length-followedUp)}</div><div class="lbl">Not Followed-up</div></div>
    `;
  }
  const byOltBody = document.getElementById('highRiskSummaryByOlt');
  if(byOltBody){
    const byOlt = {};
    sorted.forEach(u=>{
      const key = u.OLT || 'Unknown';
      if(!byOlt[key]) byOlt[key] = {total:0, followed:0};
      byOlt[key].total++;
      if((HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(u.Username)]||{}).followUp) byOlt[key].followed++;
    });
    const rowsArr = Object.entries(byOlt).sort((a,b)=>b[1].total-a[1].total);
    byOltBody.innerHTML = rowsArr.length ? rowsArr.map(([olt,v])=>`
      <tr><td>${olt}</td><td>${fmtNum(v.total)}</td><td>${fmtNum(v.followed)}</td><td>${fmtNum(v.total-v.followed)}</td></tr>
    `).join('') : `<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">No records</td></tr>`;
  }
}
// Event delegation attached ONCE (tbody innerHTML is replaced on every render, but the
// tbody element itself persists, so a single delegated listener is enough — avoids
// duplicate-listener bugs from re-attaching inside renderHighRisk()).
document.getElementById('highRiskBody').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('hrFollowBtn')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const key = normUser(uname);
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] || { username: uname, followUp:false, remarks:'' };
    rec.followUp = !rec.followUp;
    if(!rec.followUp){ rec.remarks=''; rec.followedUpBy=null; rec.followedUpByName=null; }
    else { rec.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rec.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name; }
    HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] = rec;
    fbSaveFollowup(uname, rec.followUp, rec.remarks, 'highrisk');
    renderHighRisk();
    renderSummary();
  }
});
document.getElementById('highRiskBody').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('remarksInput')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); e.target.value=''; return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const key = normUser(uname);
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] || { username: uname, followUp:true, remarks:'' };
    rec.remarks = e.target.value;
    rec.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rec.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
    HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] = rec;
    fbSaveFollowup(uname, rec.followUp, rec.remarks, 'highrisk');
    renderSummary();
  }
});

function remarksSelectHtml(uname, currentVal){
  const opts = REMARK_OPTIONS.map(o=>`<option value="${o}" ${o===currentVal?'selected':''}>${o}</option>`).join('');
  return `<select class="remarksInput" data-uname="${uname}"><option value="" ${!currentVal?'selected':''}>-- Select --</option>${opts}</select>`;
}

// ---------- Staff-wise Summary Report ----------
function renderBillingBySource(){
  const el = document.getElementById('billingBySourceKpis');
  if(!el) return;
  const totalBilled = BILLING_PAID.size;
  const fromExpiry = RAW.filter(r=> isBilled(r.username)).length;
  const fromHighRisk = (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).filter(u=> isBilled(u.Username)).length;
  const ysdSource = (YSD_DATA && YSD_DATA.length) ? YSD_DATA : (window.__YSD_DEFAULT_DATA__ || []);
  const fromYSD = ysdSource.filter(d=> isBilled(d.u)).length;
  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi blue"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalBilled)}</div><div class="lbl">Total Billing Records Imported</div></div>
    <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${fmtNum(fromExpiry)}</div><div class="lbl">Matched — Expiry Users</div></div>
    <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${fmtNum(fromHighRisk)}</div><div class="lbl">Matched — High Risk Users</div></div>
    <div class="kpi red"><div class="val">${fmtNum(fromYSD)}</div><div class="lbl">Matched — Year Start Disable</div></div>
  `;
}

function renderSummary(){
  renderBillingBySource();
  const wrap = document.getElementById('summaryPanel');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter') ? document.getElementById('oltFilter').value : 'ALL';
  const hrOltByUser = {}; // normalized username -> OLT, from curated high-risk list
  (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).forEach(u=>{ hrOltByUser[normUser(u.Username)] = u.OLT; });
  const staffStats = {}; // id -> {name, followUps, categories:{}, collected:0}
  function ensure(id, name){
    if(!staffStats[id]) staffStats[id] = { name: name||id, followUps:0, categories:{}, collected:0 };
    return staffStats[id];
  }
  // from main dataset
  RAW.forEach(r=>{
    if(oltSel!=='ALL' && r.olt!==oltSel) return;
    if(r.followUp && r.followedUpBy){
      const s = ensure(r.followedUpBy, r.followedUpByName);
      s.followUps++;
      const cat = r.remarks || 'Uncategorized';
      s.categories[cat] = (s.categories[cat]||0) + 1;
      if(BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username))){
        s.collected += (BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(r.username)] != null ? BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(r.username)] : r.revenue);
      }
    }
  });
  // from high-risk dataset
  Object.values(HR_FOLLOWUPS).forEach(rec=>{
    if(oltSel!=='ALL' && hrOltByUser[normUser(rec.username)]!==oltSel) return;
    if(rec.followUp && rec.followedUpBy){
      const s = ensure(rec.followedUpBy, rec.followedUpByName);
      s.followUps++;
      const cat = rec.remarks || 'Uncategorized';
      s.categories[cat] = (s.categories[cat]||0) + 1;
      if(BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(rec.username))){
        s.collected += (BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(rec.username)] || 0);
      }
    }
  });
  // effort hours from Firebase cache
  Object.keys(STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL).forEach(id=>{
    ensure(id, STAFF_CREDENTIALS[id] ? STAFF_CREDENTIALS[id].name : id);
  });

  // ---- Overall follow-up status (always shown, regardless of per-staff data) ----
  const scopeRows = RAW.filter(r=> oltSel==='ALL' || r.olt===oltSel);
  const totalExpiring = scopeRows.length;
  const totalFollowed = scopeRows.filter(r=>r.followUp).length;
  const scopeHR = (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).filter(u=> oltSel==='ALL' || u.OLT===oltSel);
  const hrFollowed = scopeHR.filter(u=> (HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(u.Username)]||{}).followUp).length;
  const statusHtml = `
    <div class="kpis" style="margin-bottom:14px;">
      <div class="kpi blue"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalExpiring)}</div><div class="lbl">Expiring Users (filtered)</div></div>
      <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalFollowed)}</div><div class="lbl">Followed-up</div></div>
      <div class="kpi red"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalExpiring-totalFollowed)}</div><div class="lbl">Pending Follow-up</div></div>
      <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${fmtNum(hrFollowed)}/${fmtNum(scopeHR.length)}</div><div class="lbl">High Risk Followed-up</div></div>
    </div>`;

  const ids = Object.keys(staffStats);
  if(!ids.length){
    wrap.innerHTML = statusHtml + `<div class="note">Halsam kunai staff le follow-up gareko chaina. Login garera follow-up marking start garnus.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  let html = statusHtml + `<div class="scrollx"><table><thead><tr>
    <th>Staff</th><th>Total Follow-ups</th><th>Category breakdown</th><th>Effort (hrs, aaja)</th><th>Amount Collected (NPR)</th>
  </tr></thead><tbody>`;
  ids.forEach(id=>{
    const s = staffStats[id];
    const minutes = STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL[id] || 0;
    const hrs = (minutes/60).toFixed(1);
    const cats = Object.entries(s.categories).map(([k,v])=>`<span class="cat-pill">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join(' ') || '—';
    html += `<tr>
      <td><b>${s.name}</b></td>
      <td>${fmtNum(s.followUps)}</td>
      <td>${cats}</td>
      <td>${hrs} hr</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(s.collected)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  });
  html += '</tbody></table></div>';
  wrap.innerHTML = html;
}

function fbLoadStaffTime(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  fbdb.ref(`expiryTracker/staffTime/${todayStr()}`).on('value', snap=>{
    const data = snap.val() || {};
    STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL = {};
    Object.keys(data).forEach(id=>{ STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL[id] = data[id].minutes || 0; });
    renderSummary();
  });
}
fbLoadStaffTime();

// =====================================================================
// YEAR START DISABLE MODULE
// Status (Matched/Remaining) is computed live from the shared BILLING_PAID
// set (populated by the "Billing Import" button at the top of the page) —
// so importing billing data once syncs Matched/Remaining here automatically.
// =====================================================================
function ysdInferOlt(username){
  if(/_fskgd$/i.test(username)) return 'SKGD01';
  if(/_fvmd$/i.test(username)) return 'VMAD01';
  if(/_frish$/i.test(username)) return 'RISH01';
  if(/_fdmli$/i.test(username)) return 'DMLI';
  return 'UNKNOWN';
}
function ysdStatus(d){ return isBilled(d.u) ? 'Matched' : 'Remaining'; }

function loadYSDInitial(){
  YSD_DATA = window.__YSD_DEFAULT_DATA__ || [];
}

function renderYSD(){
  const total = YSD_DATA.length;
  let matched = 0;
  YSD_DATA.forEach(d=>{ if(ysdStatus(d)==='Matched') matched++; });
  const remaining = total - matched;
  document.getElementById('ysdKpiTotal').textContent = fmtNum(total);
  document.getElementById('ysdKpiMatched').textContent = fmtNum(matched);
  document.getElementById('ysdKpiRemaining').textContent = fmtNum(remaining);
  document.getElementById('ysdKpiPct').textContent = total ? ((matched/total)*100).toFixed(1)+'%' : '–';

  const remYears = YSD_DATA.filter(d=> ysdStatus(d)==='Remaining' && d.cy).map(d=>d.cy);
  document.getElementById('ysdKpiOldest').textContent = remYears.length ? Math.min(...remYears) : '–';

  // OLT table
  const oltStats = {};
  YSD_DATA.forEach(d=>{
    const o = d.olt || 'UNKNOWN';
    if(!oltStats[o]) oltStats[o] = {total:0, matched:0};
    oltStats[o].total++;
    if(ysdStatus(d)==='Matched') oltStats[o].matched++;
  });
  const oltKeys = Object.keys(oltStats).sort((a,b)=> oltStats[b].total - oltStats[a].total);
  const oltTbody = document.getElementById('ysdOltTable');
  oltTbody.innerHTML = '';
  oltKeys.forEach(o=>{
    const s = oltStats[o];
    const rem = s.total - s.matched;
    const pct = (s.matched/s.total*100);
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td><span class="ysd-olt-pill ysd-olt-${o}">${o}</span></td>
      <td class="ysd-num">${fmtNum(s.total)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num" style="color:var(--green);font-weight:600">${fmtNum(s.matched)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num" style="color:var(--red);font-weight:600">${fmtNum(rem)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num">${pct.toFixed(1)}%</td>
      <td><div class="ysd-bar-track"><div class="ysd-bar-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div></td>
    `;
    oltTbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
  if(total){
    const pct = (matched/total*100);
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.style.fontWeight='700'; tr.style.background='#F8F8F8';
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>TOTAL</td>
      <td class="ysd-num">${fmtNum(total)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num" style="color:var(--green)">${fmtNum(matched)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num" style="color:var(--red)">${fmtNum(remaining)}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num">${pct.toFixed(1)}%</td>
      <td><div class="ysd-bar-track"><div class="ysd-bar-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div></td>
    `;
    oltTbody.appendChild(tr);
  }

  // Year chart
  const yearSet = [...new Set(YSD_DATA.filter(d=>ysdStatus(d)==='Remaining').map(d=>d.cy||'Unknown'))].sort((a,b)=>{
    if(a==='Unknown') return 1; if(b==='Unknown') return -1; return a-b;
  });
  document.getElementById('ysdRemTag').textContent = remaining + ' remaining users';
  const yearOltData = {};
  oltKeys.forEach(o=> yearOltData[o] = yearSet.map(y=> YSD_DATA.filter(d=>ysdStatus(d)==='Remaining' && (d.cy||'Unknown')===y && (d.olt||'UNKNOWN')===o).length ));

  if(typeof Chart !== 'undefined'){
    if(ysdYearChartInstance) ysdYearChartInstance.destroy();
    ysdYearChartInstance = new Chart(document.getElementById('ysdYearChart'), {
      type:'bar',
      data:{ labels: yearSet, datasets: oltKeys.map(o=>({ label:o, data: yearOltData[o], backgroundColor: YSD_OLT_COLORS[o]||'#A19F9D', stack:'s' })) },
      options:{ responsive:true, plugins:{ legend:{ position:'bottom', labels:{boxWidth:12, font:{size:11}} } },
        scales:{ x:{ stacked:true, grid:{display:false} }, y:{ stacked:true, grid:{color:'#F3F2F1'} } } }
    });
    if(ysdOltChartInstance) ysdOltChartInstance.destroy();
    ysdOltChartInstance = new Chart(document.getElementById('ysdOltChart'), {
      type:'bar',
      data:{ labels: oltKeys, datasets:[
        {label:'Matched', data: oltKeys.map(o=>oltStats[o].matched), backgroundColor:'#107C10'},
        {label:'Remaining', data: oltKeys.map(o=>oltStats[o].total-oltStats[o].matched), backgroundColor:'#D13438'}
      ]},
      options:{ responsive:true, plugins:{ legend:{ position:'bottom', labels:{boxWidth:12, font:{size:11}} } },
        scales:{ x:{ stacked:true, grid:{display:false} }, y:{ stacked:true, grid:{color:'#F3F2F1'} } } }
    });
  }

  // Filters
  const oltFilterEl = document.getElementById('ysdOltFilterLocal');
  const yearFilterEl = document.getElementById('ysdYearFilter');
  const prevOlt = oltFilterEl.value, prevYear = yearFilterEl.value;
  oltFilterEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">All OLTs</option>';
  YSD_OLT_ORDER.filter(o=>oltStats[o]).forEach(o=>{
    const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value=o; opt.textContent=o; oltFilterEl.appendChild(opt);
  });
  yearFilterEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">All Create Years</option>';
  [...new Set(YSD_DATA.map(d=>d.cy).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a-b).forEach(y=>{
    const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value=y; opt.textContent=y; yearFilterEl.appendChild(opt);
  });
  if([...oltFilterEl.options].some(o=>o.value===prevOlt)) oltFilterEl.value = prevOlt;
  if([...yearFilterEl.options].some(o=>o.value===prevYear)) yearFilterEl.value = prevYear;

  renderYSDDetail();
  renderBillingBySource();
}

function ysdGetFiltered(){
  const q = document.getElementById('ysdSearchBox').value.toLowerCase();
  const oltF = document.getElementById('ysdOltFilterLocal').value;
  const stF = document.getElementById('ysdStatusFilter').value;
  const yF = document.getElementById('ysdYearFilter').value;
  return YSD_DATA.filter(d=>{
    if(q && !d.u.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    if(oltF && d.olt !== oltF) return false;
    if(stF && ysdStatus(d) !== stF) return false;
    if(yF && String(d.cy) !== yF) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function renderYSDDetail(){
  const filtered = ysdGetFiltered();
  document.getElementById('ysdDetailCount').textContent = filtered.length + ' of ' + YSD_DATA.length + ' users';
  const tbody = document.getElementById('ysdDetailTable');
  tbody.innerHTML = '';
  const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
  filtered.slice(0,500).forEach(d=>{
    const st = ysdStatus(d);
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>${billedUsernameHtml(d.u)}</td>
      <td><span class="ysd-olt-pill ysd-olt-${d.olt}">${d.olt||'—'}</span></td>
      <td>${d.created||'—'}</td>
      <td>${d.expiry||'—'}</td>
      <td class="ysd-num">${d.rev? d.rev.toLocaleString('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:0}) : '—'}</td>
      <td class="ysd-status-${st}">${st}</td>
    `;
    frag.appendChild(tr);
  });
  tbody.appendChild(frag);
  if(filtered.length>500){
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `<td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-soft);padding:10px;">…${filtered.length-500} more rows — narrow filters or export</td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  }
}
['ysdSearchBox','ysdOltFilterLocal','ysdStatusFilter','ysdYearFilter'].forEach(id=>{
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  el.addEventListener('input', renderYSDDetail);
  el.addEventListener('change', renderYSDDetail);
});

function ysdReadWorkbook(file){
  return new Promise((resolve, reject)=>{
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e)=>{
      try{
        const wb = XLSX.read(e.target.result, {type:'array', cellDates:true});
        const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {header:1, raw:true, defval:null});
        resolve(rows);
      }catch(err){ reject(err); }
    };
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  });
}
function ysdFindHeaderRow(sheetRows, mustHaveCell){
  for(let i=0;i<Math.min(sheetRows.length,15);i++){
    const row = sheetRows[i] || [];
